# Design premises verification report (post-tour)

Approved by the operator on 2026-11-04 ("approved: design premises verification"). Read-only. It checks only the FACTUAL PREMISES of the inspector's design findings (section "(1) Design and mechanism" of each stop's inspector notes), never whether a design judgment is right: that stays with the direction session and the operator. An input to the post-tour analysis; it decides nothing.

**Sealed for the operator until their tour remarks are done** (it quotes the inspector's notes; itinerary :74).

## Method

- Pass 1 (Sonnet, 6 agents, brief `BRIEF.md`): each finding split into premises and judgment; premises verified against the files; verdict PREMISES HOLD / PARTLY HOLD / PREMISE FALSE / PURE JUDGMENT / NOT CHECKABLE HERE.
- Pass 2 (5 independent agents, brief `PASS2_BRIEF.md`): every checkable finding re-verified from scratch, trying to prove pass 1 wrong; UPHELD / AMENDED / CHANGED TO <verdict>.
- Final verdict: pass 2's where it changed it, else pass 1's. AMENDED keeps the verdict; read pass 2's column for the corrected premises or fact.
- Flag set: the tour's (all `orchestrator:` flags on except `deviation_gate`, `zero_trade_windows_not_computed`, `extra_cards_at_1a`). Checkout master 78790e5f; run_102's files and the PC's live records are not here (some agents read the partial run_102 copies under SR/tests/fixtures/ and say so in the row).
- Raw files: `p_batch_01.md` to `p_batch_06.md`, `pass2_*.md`, each with a "Not checked" list.
- Incidents: one pass-2 agent's two repo-wide read-only searches may have passed through trading-bot/local_data/ (it printed nothing from there; the sealed store local_data/holdout_sealed/ is not in this checkout). One pass-2 change (03a-5) rests on the committed config, flagged in its row.
